```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a)^2)

The optimizer combines:
- Linear-programming best-response updates for the bilinear convolution peak.
- Smooth max / annealed log-sum-exp Adam gradient polishing.
- Peak-focused subgradient polishing.
- A short simulated-annealing local polish.
- Multi-start over several lengths and structured initializations.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import linprog


def fft_convolve(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    need = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    if need <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, size)
    return out[:need]


def conv_square(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    return out[:need]


def score_fast(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    n = a.size
    c = conv_square(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s))


def score_sum_s(a):
    """Assumes sum(a) = sqrt(2*n). Returns max coefficient."""
    c = conv_square(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(np.max(c))


def rescale(a, s_target):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a.copy()
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.shape, s_target / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (s_target / s)


def softmax_logits(theta, s_target):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    z = theta - float(np.max(theta))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(theta.shape, s_target / max(1, theta.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e * (s_target / s)


def grad_from_weights(a, w):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return a.copy()
    conv = fft_convolve(a[::-1], w)
    return 2.0 * conv[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def resample_seq(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)


def make_inits(n, rng, provided=None):
    inits = []

    if provided is not None and len(provided) > 0:
        inits.append(resample_seq(provided, n).copy())

    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    center = (n - 1) / 2.0
    scale = max(1.0, float(n - 1))
    d = np.abs(i - center) / scale

    for s in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        a = np.exp(s * (0.5 - d) + 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n))
        inits.append(np.maximum(a, 1e-6))

    x = i / max(1.0, float(n - 1))
    fourier_specs = [(2, 0.5), (2, 1.0), (4, 0.7), (6, 0.5)]
    for k, amp in fourier_specs:
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        a = 1.0 + amp * math.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + phase)
        a = a + 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        inits.append(np.maximum(a, 1e-6))

    for _ in range(2):
        a = 1.0 + 0.4 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        f = np.fft.rfft(a)
        freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
        f = f / (1.0 + 5.0 * freq)
        a = np.real(np.fft.irfft(f, n))
        a = 1.0 + 0.3 * a
        inits.append(np.maximum(a, 1e-6))

    for _ in range(2):
        a = np.full(n, 0.01, dtype=np.float64)
        low = max(8, int(math.sqrt(n) * 0.8))
        high = max(low + 1, int(math.sqrt(n) * 2.5) + 1)
        k = int(rng.integers(low, high))
        u = rng.beta(1.5, 1.5, k)
        pos = np.unique((u * n).astype(int))
        pos = pos[(pos >= 0) & (pos < n)]
        if pos.size > 0:
            w = rng.uniform(0.3, 2.0, pos.size)
            a[pos] += w
        inits.append(a)

    return inits


def pool_key(item):
    return item[0]


def add_to_pool(pool, score, seq, limit=20):
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return pool
    pool.append((float(score), np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    if len(pool) > limit:
        pool.sort(key=pool_key)
        pool = pool[:limit]
    return pool


def lp_best_response(a, s_target, time_limit=5.0):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return None, np.inf
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    rows = 2 * n - 1

    A = np.zeros((rows, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A[:, n] = -1.0
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = a

    c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c[n] = 1.0

    b_ub = np.zeros(rows, dtype=np.float64)

    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.array([s_target], dtype=np.float64)

    bounds = [(0.0, float(s_target)) for _ in range(n)]
    bounds.append((0.0, None))

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(max(0.1, time_limit))}
        )
        if res.success:
            g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
            t = float(res.x[n])
            if np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                g = np.clip(g, 0.0, s_target)
                return g, t
    except Exception:
        pass

    return None, np.inf


def lp_refine(a_init, time_limit, rng=None, max_iters=40, lp_time=2.5, verbose=False):
    n = len(a_init)
    s_target = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = rescale(a_init, s_target)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_sum_s(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    stagn = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit and it < max_iters:
        tleft = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if tleft < 2.0:
            break

        g, _ = lp_best_response(a, s_target, time_limit=min(lp_time, max(1.0, 0.25 * tleft)))
        it += 1

        if g is None:
            break

        g = rescale(g, s_target)

        cand = None
        cand_score = best_score
        for eta in (0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00):
            x = (1.0 - eta) * a + eta * g
            x = rescale(x, s_target)
            sc = score_sum_s(x)
            if sc < cand_score:
                cand_score = sc
                cand = x.copy()

        if cand is not None:
            a = cand
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = cand_score
            stagn = 0
        else:
            stagn += 1
            if rng is not None:
                a = rescale(a + 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n), s_target)
            if stagn > 12:
                break

        if verbose and it % 5 == 0:
            print(f"    LP iter={it}, best={best_score:.6f}, elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    return best, best_score


def gradient_adam(a_init, time_limit, rng=None, mode="softmax", base_lr=None,
                  tau_start=None, tau_end=None, verbose=False):
    n = len(a_init)
    s_target = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)

    a0 = rescale(a_init, s_target)
    maxa = float(np.max(a0)) if a0.size else 1.0
    floor = max(1e-12 * max(maxa, 1e-6), 1e-300)
    a0 = np.maximum(a0, floor)

    if base_lr is None:
        base_lr = 0.25 if mode == "softmax" else 0.08

    theta = np.log(a0 / s_target)
    theta = theta - float(np.max(theta))

    best_theta = theta.copy()
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_score = score_sum_s(best_a)

    m1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    bias1 = 0.0
    bias2 = 0.0
    step = 0
    orig_lr = float(base_lr)
    lr = float(base_lr)

    if mode == "softmax" and tau_start is None:
        c0 = conv_square(a0)
        cmax0 = float(np.max(c0))
        spread = max(0.05, cmax0 - float(np.mean(c0)))
        tau_start = 0.25 * spread
        tau_end = max(0.001, 0.005 * spread)

    if mode == "peak":
        tol_start = 0.05
        tol_end = 0.0005
        top_k = 8

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = softmax_logits(theta, s_target)
    c = conv_square(a)
    bad = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / float(max(1e-12, time_limit)))
        cmax = float(np.max(c))

        if mode == "softmax":
            tau = tau_start * (tau_end / tau_start) ** frac
            z = (c - cmax) / tau
            z = np.minimum(z, 0.0)
            e = np.exp(z)
            esum = float(np.sum(e))
            if esum <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(esum):
                e = np.ones_like(c)
                esum = float(c.size)
            w = e / esum
        else:
            tol = tol_start * (tol_end / tol_start) ** frac
            active = np.flatnonzero(c >= cmax - tol)
            if active.size == 0:
                active = np.argsort(c)[-top_k:]
            elif active.size > 32:
                order = np.argsort(c[active])[::-1][:32]
                active = active[order]
            w = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=np.float64)
            w[active] = 1.0 / float(len(active))

        g = grad_from_weights(a, w)
        inner = float(np.dot(a, g))
        gt = a * g - (a / s_target) * inner

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gt)):
            theta = best_theta.copy()
            a = softmax_logits(theta, s_target)
            c = conv_square(a)
            m1.fill(0.0)
            m2.fill(0.0)
            bias1 = 0.0
            bias2 = 0.0
            step = 0
            bad = 50
            continue

        step += 1
        m1 = beta1 * m1 + (1.0 - beta1) * gt
        m2 = beta2 * m2 + (1.0 - beta2) * (gt * gt)
        bias1 = beta1 * bias1 + (1.0 - beta1)
        bias2 = beta2 * bias2 + (1.0 - beta2)

        m1h = m1 / bias1
        m2h = m2 / bias2
        lr = orig_lr / math.sqrt(1.0 + 0.02 * step)
        delta = lr * m1h / (np.sqrt(m2h) + eps)
        theta_new = theta - delta

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta_new)):
            theta = best_theta.copy()
            a = softmax_logits(theta, s_target)
            c = conv_square(a)
            m1.fill(0.0)
            m2.fill(0.0)
            bias1 = 0.0
            bias2 = 0.0
            step = 0
            bad = 50
            continue

        theta = theta_new
        a_new = softmax_logits(theta, s_target)
        c_new = conv_square(a_new)
        score_new = float(np.max(c_new))
        a = a_new
        c = c_new

        if math.isfinite(score_new):
            if score_new < best_score:
                best_score = score_new
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                best_theta = theta.copy()
                bad = 0

            if score_new > best_score + 0.02:
                bad += 1
            else:
                bad = max(0, bad - 1)

            if bad > 60:
                if rng is not None:
                    theta = best_theta + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                else:
                    theta = best_theta.copy()
                a = softmax_logits(theta, s_target)
                c = conv_square(a)
                m1.fill(0.0)
                m2.fill(0.0)
                bias1 = 0.0
                bias2 = 0.0
                step = 0
                orig_lr = max(0.02, 0.7 * orig_lr)
                bad = 0

        if verbose and step % 2000 == 0:
            print(f"    grad mode={mode}, step={step}, score={score_new:.6f}, best={best_score:.6f}")

    return best_a, best_score


def make_peak_move(a, rng):
    n = len(a)
    c = conv_square(a)
    k = int(np.argmax(c))
    lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
    hi = min(n - 1, k)
    if hi < lo:
        return a

    idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
    jdx = k - idx
    contrib = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    contrib[idx] = a[idx] * a[jdx]

    order = np.argsort(contrib)[::-1]
    top = order[:max(1, min(8, n))]
    a[top] *= np.exp(rng.normal(-0.04, 0.012, size=top.size))

    rest = np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), top)
    if rest.size > 0:
        inc_n = min(8, rest.size)
        inc = rng.choice(rest, size=inc_n, replace=False)
        a[inc] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.04, 0.012, size=inc_n))

    return a


def make_segment_move(a, rng):
    n = len(a)
    width = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 8)))
    if width <= 0:
        return a
    width = min(width, n)
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
    a[start:start + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.025, size=width))
    return a


def sa_polish(a_init, time_limit, rng):
    n = len(a_init)
    s_target = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = rescale(a_init, s_target)

    current_score = score_fast(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current_score

    temp = 0.0005
    cool = 0.999
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        cand = a.copy()
        r = rng.random()

        if r < 0.45:
            cand = make_peak_move(cand, rng)
        elif r < 0.75:
            cand = make_segment_move(cand, rng)
        elif r < 0.90:
            cand = make_segment_move(cand, rng)
            cand = make_segment_move(cand, rng)
        else:
            cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.004, size=n))

        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
        cand = rescale(cand, s_target)
        new_score = score_fast(cand)

        if math.isfinite(new_score):
            if new_score <= current_score:
                a = cand
                current_score = new_score
                if new_score < best_score:
                    best_score = new_score
                    best = cand.copy()
            else:
                delta = new_score - current_score
                ratio = delta / max(1e-12, temp)
                if ratio <= 50.0:
                    if rng.random() < math.exp(-ratio):
                        a = cand
                        current_score = new_score

        temp = max(1e-6, temp * cool)

    return best, best_score


def optimize_candidate(a_init, time_limit, rng=None, do_lp=True, verbose=False):
    best = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_score = score_fast(best)
    n = len(best)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if do_lp and 32 <= n <= 768:
        lp_budget = min(0.35 * float(time_limit), 150.0)
        if time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0) > lp_budget and lp_budget > 10.0:
            a2, sc2 = lp_refine(best, lp_budget, rng=rng, max_iters=25,
                                lp_time=2.0, verbose=verbose)
            if sc2 < best_score:
                best = a2
                best_score = sc2

    t1 = time.monotonic()
    rem = float(time_limit) - (t1 - t0)
    if rem > 20.0:
        grad_budget = min(0.50 * float(time_limit), 350.0, 0.60 * rem)
        if grad_budget > 10.0:
            a2, sc2 = gradient_adam(best, grad_budget, rng=rng, mode="softmax",
                                    base_lr=0.25, verbose=verbose)
            if sc2 < best_score:
                best = a2
                best_score = sc2

    t2 = time.monotonic()
    rem = float(time_limit) - (t2 - t0)
    if rem > 20.0:
        peak_budget = min(0.25 * float(time_limit), 160.0, 0.70 * rem)
        if peak_budget > 10.0:
            a2, sc2 = gradient_adam(best, peak_budget, rng=rng, mode="peak",
                                    base_lr=0.08, verbose=verbose)
            if sc2 < best_score:
                best = a2
                best_score = sc2

    final_score = score_fast(best)
    if math.isfinite(final_score) and final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score

    return best, best_score


def try_true_sequence(lst):
    try:
        if "evaluate_sequence" in globals():
            return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    if budget_s is None:
        budget_s = 1800
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_global + float(budget_s) - 30.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    provided = None
    if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
        raw = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
        if raw is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if arr.size > 0:
                provided = arr

    pool = []

    if provided is not None:
        sc_prov = score_fast(provided)
        pool = add_to_pool(pool, sc_prov, provided)

    pool = add_to_pool(pool, 2.0, np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))

    base_n = len(provided) if provided is not None else 256
    length_set = set()
    for L in (base_n,
              max(64, base_n // 2),
              min(2048, 2 * base_n),
              256, 384, 512, 768, 1024):
        if 32 <= L <= 2048:
            length_set.add(int(L))

    lengths = sorted(length_set)

    print("Generating initial candidates...")
    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
            break
        s_target = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
        inits = make_inits(n, rng, provided)
        for init in inits:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            a = rescale(init, s_target)
            sc = score_sum_s(a)
            pool = add_to_pool(pool, sc, a)

        print(f"  n={n}, pool_size={len(pool)}, best={pool[0][0]:.6f}" if pool else f"  n={n}, pool empty")

    if not pool:
        pool.append((2.0, np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)))

    pool.sort(key=pool_key)
    top = pool[:8]

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf
    for sc, seq in top[:2]:
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    print("Quick refinement of top candidates...")
    alloc_list = [240.0, 180.0, 120.0, 80.0, 50.0, 30.0, 20.0, 20.0]
    for idx, (sc, seq) in enumerate(top):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
            break
        alloc = alloc_list[idx] if idx < len(alloc_list) else 20.0
        alloc = min(alloc, max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 120.0))
        if sc > best_score + 0.05:
            alloc = min(alloc, 40.0)
        if alloc < 15.0:
            break

        n = len(seq)
        do_lp = (n <= 768)
        print(f"  candidate {idx}: n={n}, local={sc:.6f}, alloc={alloc:.0f}s")

        cand, score = optimize_candidate(seq, alloc, rng=rng, do_lp=do_lp, verbose=False)
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_seq = cand.copy()
            print(f"    improved to {best_score:.6f}")

        pool = add_to_pool(pool, score, cand, limit=20)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0

    print(f"Final polishing best candidate (n={len(best_seq)}, score={best_score:.6f})...")
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        grad_budget = min(0.45 * rem, 320.0)
        if grad_budget > 20.0:
            cand, score = gradient_adam(best_seq, grad_budget, rng=rng, mode="softmax",
                                        base_lr=0.25, verbose=True)
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_seq = cand.copy()

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        peak_budget = min(0.30 * rem, 200.0)
        if peak_budget > 20.0:
            cand, score = gradient_adam(best_seq, peak_budget, rng=rng, mode="peak",
                                        base_lr=0.08, verbose=True)
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_seq = cand.copy()

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        sa_budget = min(rem, 120.0)
        if sa_budget > 20.0:
            cand, score = sa_polish(best_seq, sa_budget, rng)
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_seq = cand.copy()

    final_options = []

    pool.sort(key=pool_key)
    for sc, seq in pool[:6]:
        vals = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        lst = []
        for y in vals:
            y = float(y)
            if not math.isfinite(y):
                y = 0.0
            if y < 0.0:
                y = 0.0
            if y > 1000.0:
                y = 1000.0
            lst.append(y)
        ts = try_true_sequence(lst)
        final_options.append((ts, sc, lst))

    best_list = []
    for y in np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64):
        y = float(y)
        if not math.isfinite(y):
            y = 0.0
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        if y > 1000.0:
            y = 1000.0
        best_list.append(y)
    ts_best = try_true_sequence(best_list)
    final_options.append((ts_best, best_score, best_list))

    uni = [1.0] * 256
    ts_uni = try_true_sequence(uni)
    final_options.append((ts_uni, 2.0, uni))

    result_list = None
    result_true = np.inf
    result_local = np.inf
    for ts, sc, lst in final_options:
        if not lst:
            continue
        if ts < result_true or (not math.isfinite(ts) and sc < result_local):
            result_true = ts
            result_local = sc
            result_list = lst

    if result_list is None:
        result_list = [1.0] * 256

    final_list = []
    for y in result_list:
        y = float(y)
        if not math.isfinite(y):
            y = 0.0
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        if y > 1000.0:
            y = 1000.0
        final_list.append(y)

    print(f"FINAL: n={len(final_list)}, local={result_local:.6f}, true={result_true:.6f}")
    return final_list
```
